Any design section a bullet names and this issue does not quote is read there.

Design section 12, verbatim (C1 to C5 are what a bullet means by "C1", "C2 and C3", "C4" or "C5"):

*Common evidence (every library entry; values in its first bullet; the shared tools are built in the scaffold entry, whose evidence carries their contracts; scratch space is mktemp -d outside the repo).* C1 BASE from tools/testcount.sh before any edit and AFTER at least BASE + F. C2 tools/comment-rule.sh exits 0 with files above 0. C3 tools/ci-chain.sh exits 0. C4 for each value export added: the barrel test's list gains exactly those names (compared with Object.keys(index)), api-report:update then check:api-report exit 0 and git diff --stat -- etc is non-empty; for each type added to index.ts the entry lists the names and check:api-report prints no warning of any kind (ae-forgotten-export, ae-missing-release-tag or any other); none, and the diff is empty, otherwise; a field, prop or signature change is named in the entry and appears in the report diff. C5 mutants: node tools/mutate-check.mjs tools/mutants/ENTRY.json --planned N (ENTRY is the entry's id). A descriptor's differential is differential(NAME, ...) in test/support/differential.ts; the global gate greps test/*.test.ts for that literal.

*Barrel test.* Its title carries the count ('exactly the 43 runtime values, no more, no fewer'). The entry that adds value exports edits the title and the sorted list in the same commit, renaming and never deleting the test: resolver 50, permissions 51, look tuning 54, keepMounted 55, row numbers 56, attach 60, header buttons 62; the release-candidate entry asserts 62 and edits no title. Every other entry leaves the file's title and list alone.

*Brief rule for every worker (both repos' CLAUDE.md):* comments say why, a constraint the code cannot show; no provenance (ticket or queue ids, dates, owner names); past-bug stories go to CHANGELOG.md or the commit message; design notes go in DESIGN.md, never a link to a tracker. Pins live in the entry that changes the behaviour, committed with its first tests; a flipped pin is renamed, never deleted.

Declarations for evidence bullet 18 (verbatim; the one source of these shapes, equivalent to design section 6):

```ts
type SourceLayer = 'library' | 'workspace' | 'template' | 'host' | 'instance' | 'user' | 'derived' | 'forced'
// cascade order, lowest to highest: library < workspace < template < host < instance < user; 'derived' marks a value filled from
// another resolved field, 'forced' one overridden by a rule C1-C6 (the last step, so it wins over every layer)
type SettingValues = {
  closable: boolean; parkable: boolean; draggable: boolean; crossable: boolean; placeable: boolean; resizable: boolean
  formFactors: readonly FormFactor[]                       // non-empty
  clearable: boolean; hostCreatable: boolean; multiple: boolean
  maxInstances: number | 'unlimited'                       // integer >= 1
  listed: boolean
  startVisibility: 'live' | 'parked'; hideStickiness: 'sticky' | 'until-room'; hideWhenLast: 'allow' | 'refuse'
  capacityReturn: 'auto' | 'manual'; returnPriority: number; overflow: Overflow; evictionRank: number
  evictionOrder: 'farthest' | 'nearest' | 'leftmost' | 'rightmost'
  revealOnScroll: 'edges' | 'any' | 'never'; scrollOnOpen: ScrollOnOpen; scrollOnMove: ScrollOnMove; scrollOnDrag: ScrollOnDrag
  toggleWhenClosed: 'ignore' | 'reopen'; showWhenClosed: 'refuse' | 'reopen'
  viewState: 'keep' | 'reset-on-park'; keepMounted: boolean
  placeOnAdd: AddPlacement; placeOnShow: AddPlacement | 'in-place'; placeOnReturn: AddPlacement | 'in-place'
  placeFallback: 'row-start' | 'row-end' | 'parkable-start' | 'parkable-end' | 'after-fixed'
  undoPlacement: 'old-slot' | AddPlacement
  layer: 'base' | 'raised'; enterFrom: 'auto' | 'left' | 'right' | 'anchor'; exitTo: 'auto' | 'left' | 'right'
  closeExit: 'down' | 'left' | 'right'; animated: boolean
  formFactor: FormFactor; title: string
  pinning: Pinning; pinningWhenNotLive: PinningWhenNotLive
  persist: 'saved' | 'session'; rememberLayout: boolean; restoreVisibility: 'saved' | 'live' | 'parked'
  afterClose: 'stay-closed' | 'return-next-launch'
  undoWindowMs: number; reportClose: boolean; snapshot: 'both' | 'park' | 'close' | 'never'
}   // 49 keys: the catalog's 61 names less headerButtons and the 11 tuning leaves
type DragToCloseTuning = { distanceFraction: number; minDistancePx: number; flickMinDistancePx: number; flickVelocity: number; cancelVelocity: number; directionLockPx: number; snapBack: { stiffness: number; damping: number } }
type SlideTuning = { durationSec: number; ease: readonly [number, number, number, number]; closeExitSeconds: number }   // ease x1 and x2 in [0, 1]
type EdgeScrollTuning = { zonePx: number; maxPxPerSec: number; reducedStepMs: number }
type PanelTuning = {   // the per-panel leaves; each may also be set on the workspace
  dragToClose?: { [K in keyof DragToCloseTuning]?: DragToCloseTuning[K] }; slide?: { [K in keyof SlideTuning]?: SlideTuning[K] }
  hopFraction?: number; edgeScroll?: { [K in keyof EdgeScrollTuning]?: EdgeScrollTuning[K] }
}
type WorkspaceTuning = PanelTuning & {   // plus the seven workspace-only leaves
  rowGap?: number; saveQuietMs?: number; gestureLockMs?: number; returnTieBreak?: 'latest-first' | 'earliest-first' | 'row-order'
  dropTieBreak?: 'leftmost' | 'rightmost'; rowGesture?: 'step' | 'free' | 'off'; initialScroll?: 'start' | 'end' | 'root' | { panel: PanelId }
}
type ResolvedPanelTuning = { dragToClose: DragToCloseTuning; slide: SlideTuning; hopFraction: number; edgeScroll: EdgeScrollTuning }
type ResolvedWorkspaceTuning = ResolvedPanelTuning & { rowGap: number; saveQuietMs: number; gestureLockMs: number; returnTieBreak: 'latest-first' | 'earliest-first' | 'row-order'
  dropTieBreak: 'leftmost' | 'rightmost'; rowGesture: 'step' | 'free' | 'off'; initialScroll: 'start' | 'end' | 'root' | { panel: PanelId } }
type ResolvedPanelSettings = {   // deeply frozen; every key present after a resolve except title
  readonly [K in Exclude<keyof SettingValues, 'title'>]: SettingValues[K]
} & { readonly title?: string; readonly tuning: ResolvedPanelTuning; readonly headerButtons: { readonly [id: string]: HeaderButton } }
type ViewPatch = {   // exactly the keys a view may set on its own panel; any other key is not-view-settable; null deletes the instance value
  title?: string | null; listed?: boolean | null; layer?: 'base' | 'raised' | null; formFactor?: FormFactor | null
  placeOnAdd?: { anchor: AnchorRequest & { panel: PanelId } } | null; enterFrom?: 'anchor' | null
}
type SettingConflict = {   // recorded only for a template, host or instance layer that asked for what a rule or a scope forbids
  readonly key: string                                        // the catalog name that was asked (a tuning leaf is dotted: 'tuning.rowGap')
  readonly layer: 'template' | 'host' | 'instance'            // the SourceLayer that asked
  readonly kind: 'forced' | 'wrong-scope' | 'not-view-settable'
  readonly asked: unknown; readonly applied: unknown          // the asked value; the value the resolved settings hold instead
  readonly rule?: 'C1' | 'C2' | 'C3' | 'C4' | 'C5' | 'C6'    // present when kind is 'forced'
  readonly forcedBy?: string                                  // the catalog name whose value triggered the rule (C1 'parkable', C2 'closable', C3 'persist', C4 'formFactors', C6 'viewState'); absent for C5
  readonly message: string
}
type DeferredPin = { by: 'pin' | 'unpin' } | { parkable: boolean }
type WorkspaceStatus = 'detached' | 'loading' | 'ready' | 'disposed'
type ListFilter = { status?: PanelStatus | readonly PanelStatus[]; listed?: boolean }
interface PanelState {   // readonly snapshot of one panel
  readonly id: PanelId; readonly templateId: string; readonly status: PanelStatus; readonly held: boolean
  readonly engineId: string | null; readonly index: number | null      // null while the panel has no engine entry (pending or closed)
  readonly title: string | undefined; readonly url: string; readonly params: JsonValue | undefined
  readonly formFactor: FormFactor; readonly listed: boolean; readonly settings: ResolvedPanelSettings
  readonly pinned: boolean; readonly deferredPin: DeferredPin | null
}
type PanelSnapshot = { readonly version: number; readonly status: WorkspaceStatus; readonly panels: readonly PanelState[] }
type PanelReadHandle = {   // the five read-only members every handle and view handle has
  readonly id: PanelId; readonly templateId: string; getState(): PanelState
  getSettings(): { value: ResolvedPanelSettings; source: Record<string, SourceLayer>; conflicts: SettingConflict[]; notes: SettingNote[]; userAnchor?: AnchorRequest }; subscribe(l: () => void): () => void
}
```

### 3. phonux-engine-permission-settings (phonux-lib)

**Enforce permission, undo-window, report and snapshot settings in the engine**

Intent: The engine reads the 14 permission and lifecycle settings it owns: closable, parkable, draggable, crossable, placeable, resizable, formFactors, clearable, listed, formFactor, title, undoWindowMs, reportClose, snapshot. Adds PanelWindow.settings and key, effective(), applyTemplate and the slot-context leaf; clearAll keeps locked entries.

Prerequisites: phonux-settings-catalog-and-resolver

Touches:
- panelWindow.ts
- panelRowEntry.ts
- panelSettings.ts
- panelSlotContext.ts
- PanelRow.tsx
- panels/useProvidePanels.ts
- panels/usePanelClosing.ts
- index.ts
- DESIGN.md
- test/pins-permission.test.ts
- test/settings-enforcement-permissions.test.ts
- test/source-invariants.test.ts
- test/boundary.test.ts
- test/phonux-barrel.test.ts
- tools/mutants/phonux-engine-permission-settings.json
- etc/*.api.md

Evidence required:
1. C1 with F = 70. New root files (panelSlotContext.ts) raise MIN_LIBRARY_FILES in test/boundary.test.ts in the same commit. Every test in this entry that mounts useProvidePanels or usePanelManager builds its input through `panelsInput(...)` or `usePanelManagerWith(...)` from test/support/mount-harness.ts (the scaffold entry's helpers) and never writes a storedMaxPanels property or positional null itself; the retire-max-panels entry then edits that one file.
2. Interfaces this entry produces (signatures in code, later entries consume them): PanelWindow gains `settings?: ResolvedPanelSettings` and `key?: string` (PanelRowEntry too; no other field); `key` is written by applyTemplate('create') only: a hand-built entry has none and readers use `p.key ?? p.id`; panelSettings.ts exports `effective(p: PanelWindow | PanelRowEntry): ResolvedPanelSettings` (p.settings, else legacy-derived values: overflow 'park' when collapsible, 'scroll' otherwise) and `applyTemplate(entry: PanelWindow, resolved: ResolvedPanelSettings, mode: 'create' | 'reconfigure'): PanelWindow`; panelSlotContext.ts (a new leaf) holds the PanelSlotContext that PanelRow provided privately (PanelRow.tsx:37), with a `closable` member, so DragToClose can read it without importing PanelRow (PanelRow imports PanelRowSlot, which imports dragToClose: a cycle); usePanelReorderEligible and the other slot hooks stay exported under their names.
3. Pins first, in test/pins-permission.test.ts, committed before any source change and green at that commit (`git show --stat PIN-COMMIT` lists only test/ paths; the entry's handoff names the commit): PIN movePanel across a parked panel: [P, X parked, Q] moving P right gives [X, Q, P]; KNOWN-BAD:1 initialEntries seeded { locked: true, collapsible: true } is removed by clearAll; KNOWN-BAD:3 two closes before a re-render undo in the wrong order. `grep -rhoE "test\(.KNOWN-BAD:[0-9]" test | sort -u | wc -l` (distinct pin titles, so a pinned string repeated inside one pin counts once) prints 2 at that commit.
4. Owned differentials: test/settings-enforcement-permissions.test.ts runs one differential per owned name through the real pure function or hook; its covered-name set equals the list of 14 (non-empty; a missing name fails).
5. Permission effects: closable:false leaves entries reference-equal on close, closingIds unchanged, no onPanelsClosed call, clearAll keeps it; the root resolves closable:false (C5) and its close is refused; draggable:false makes movePanel return the same array for that source while another panel's drag still crosses it; placeable:false makes reanchorPanel return the same array; resizable:false or a disallowed tier makes setFormFactor return the same array and onPanelResize never fires; clearable:false survives clearAll while another panel is removed; listed:false hides it from panelList.
6. crossable: movePanel returns the same array when any array slot between source and target holds a non-crossable entry, parked ones included ([P, X parked non-crossable, Q], moving P right, is a barrier; the pin showed [X, Q, P]); a PanelRow mount shows usePanelNeighborWidths truncated at the non-crossable entry; a parkable non-crossable panel's index relative to its neighbours is unchanged under 200 seeded moves of others, live and parked, and likewise a draggable:false fixed column.
7. undoWindowMs, reportClose, snapshot: undoWindowMs:0 is absent from recentlyClosed, undoClose leaves it, the capture spy sees exactly one evict, a second close in the same turn is a no-op, an earlier window end is unchanged; a 2 s value inside a 10 s window leaves the end unchanged, a 30 s value extends it. reportClose:false: onPanelsClosed not called and onPanelsRestored lists only reported, restored ids. snapshot never: 0 capture and 0 evict calls (positive control: both >= 1 under both); `snapshot: 'park'` and `snapshot: 'close'` each differ from `snapshot: 'both'` (the default; the catalog's values are both, park, close, never), and `never` is the off case above.
8. The KNOWN-BAD:1 flip: it now asserts the fixed behaviour (clearAll keeps a locked parkable entry) under a name without the prefix; `grep -rhoE "test\(.KNOWN-BAD:[0-9]" test | sort -u | wc -l` (distinct pin titles, so a pinned string repeated inside one pin counts once) prints 1 (the close-order pin remains for the operations entry). PanelWindow.key is written by applyTemplate('create') only and never by reconfigure; appendPanel, restorePanel and hydrateWorkspaceState never write it, because the scaffold's corpus deepEquals their 0.1.0 outputs, which carry no key. Compat caller seeding { locked: true, collapsible: true }: clearAll keeps it and usePanelCloseEligible is false for it. Corpus intact after this entry: the scaffold's corpus test replays appendPanel, restorePanel and hydrateWorkspaceState with replayed === corpus.length > 0, every output deepEquals the vendored output and `'key' in entry` is false for every entry of every replayed output (so appendPanel's output is deepEqual to the corpus; the typecheck test passes too); a hand-built entry reads as `p.key ?? p.id` in the uniqueness checks; mutant: appendPanel writes key = id; the corpus test goes red.
9. Writer modes: create over at least 300 resolver-random entries: locked === !closable, collapsible === parkable, listed, layer, formFactor agree, anchoredEnter === (enterFrom === 'anchor'). reconfigure writes only locked, listed, layer, anchoredEnter, title, settings, instance and (collapsible for the live parkable flip) and leaves formFactor, size, anchor, live, hostData, key reference-equal on a person-resized tablet entry and on a dragged entry; mutant: reconfigure writes formFactor from settings; red. test/source-invariants.test.ts gains: locked and collapsible are assigned from settings only inside applyTemplate (plus the 0.1.0 literals in appendPanel and createHistoryPanel) and `key` is assigned only inside applyTemplate's create mode (appendPanel has no key write), checked by an AST scan of panelWindow.ts, panelRowEntry.ts, panels/*.ts for assignments and object-literal writes to those two keys; and every guard in panelWindow.ts, usePanelManager.ts and useProvidePanels.ts that reads `.collapsible` for a permission decision is listed in an allow-list by function name, any other read of `.collapsible` outside it fails; a planted extra writer in a scratch copy turns it red.
10. Legacy defaults (moved here from the resolver entry, which cannot see entry fields): effective(entry) for a hand-built entry without settings returns overflow 'park' when collapsible and 'scroll' when not (the two literal cases) and every other key at its library default; applyTemplate('create') over the library defaults equals appendPanel's literals (locked false, collapsible true, listed true, layer base, anchoredEnter false, formFactor phone); with settings present effective returns them unchanged (reference-equal). Mutant: the legacy overflow default changed from 'park' to 'scroll'; red.
11. usePanelCapabilities: a mount test inside a row slot returns exactly closable, draggable, placeable, resizable, formFactors (Object.keys asserted); outside a row it returns library defaults; usePanelReorderEligible follows draggable.
12. Design section 12, verbatim (C1 to C5 are what a bullet means by "C1", "C2 and C3", "C4" or "C5"):

*Common evidence (every library entry; values in its first bullet; the shared tools are built in the scaffold entry, whose evidence carries their contracts; scratch space is mktemp -d outside the repo).* C1 BASE from tools/testcount.sh before any edit and AFTER at least BASE + F. C2 tools/comment-rule.sh exits 0 with files above 0. C3 tools/ci-chain.sh exits 0. *Barrel test.* Its title carries the count ('exactly the 43 runtime values, no more, no fewer'). The entry that adds value exports edits the title and the sorted list in the same commit, renaming and never deleting the test: resolver 50, permissions 51, look tuning 54, keepMounted 55, row numbers 56, attach 60, header buttons 62; the release-candidate entry asserts 62 and edits no title. Every other entry leaves the file's title and list alone.

*Brief rule for every worker (both repos' CLAUDE.md):* comments say why, a constraint the code cannot show; no provenance (ticket or queue ids, dates, owner names); past-bug stories go to CHANGELOG.md or the commit message; design notes go in DESIGN.md, never a link to a tracker. Pins live in the entry that changes the behaviour, committed with its first tests; a flipped pin is renamed, never deleted.

### 4. phonux-engine-visibility-settings (phonux-lib)

**Enforce hold, return and eviction-rank settings and the timing tuning**

Intent: startVisibility, hideStickiness (held map, until-room threshold), hideWhenLast, capacityReturn, returnPriority, evictionRank, evictionOrder, viewState and the four timing keys saveQuietMs, gestureLockMs, returnTieBreak, dropTieBreak. One recordPark choke point; engine hold API and the note reporter stated as signatures. Park levers act on park-policy panels.

Prerequisites: phonux-engine-permission-settings

Touches:
- panels/usePanelManager.ts
- panels/useProvidePanels.ts
- panels/useRowScrollGesture.ts
- panels/useWorkspacePersistence.ts
- panelWindow.ts
- PanelRow.tsx
- panelSettings.ts
- test/pins-visibility.test.ts
- test/settings-enforcement-visibility.test.ts
- test/panel-manager-collapse-decisions.test.ts
- test/rowconstants-drift.test.ts
- test/source-invariants.test.ts
- tools/mutants/phonux-engine-visibility-settings.json
- etc/*.api.md

Evidence required:
1. C1 with F = 75. Every test in this entry that mounts useProvidePanels or usePanelManager builds its input through `panelsInput(...)` or `usePanelManagerWith(...)` from test/support/mount-harness.ts (the scaffold entry's helpers) and never writes a storedMaxPanels property or positional null itself; the retire-max-panels entry then edits that one file. Any input this entry adds to usePanelManager goes in one optional trailing options object (the first entry that needs one creates it; later entries add optional fields), never a new positional parameter, so test/support/mount-harness.ts's fixed call keeps typechecking without an edit here.
2. Interfaces this entry produces. UsePanelManagerResult gains: `holdPanel(id: string, kind: 'sticky' | 'until-room'): boolean` (parks id, records the hold, false for an unknown id, a fixed column or an id already parked and held), `releaseHold(id: string): boolean` (drops the hold mark only, never touches live), `heldIds: ReadonlyMap of id to 'sticky' or { freeAfterPark: number }` (session only), `wouldEmptyRow(ids: readonly string[]): boolean` (true iff no other present panel, fixed columns counted, remains once ids are parked). `markHeld(id: string, kind: 'sticky' | 'until-room'): boolean` records the hold of an id that is already parked (false for a live or unknown id); every creation path calls it in the commit that adds a panel created parked, and applyTemplate('create') (permission entry) gains the write of `live` from the resolved startVisibility, so a differential has both functions to call. PanelsInput gains `onNote?: (note: SettingNote) => void` and `tuning?` (a partial WorkspaceTuning); a note the engine raises (protection and park-lever notes) goes only through onNote and never throws. The manager's hide() later calls holdPanel; would-empty-row is the manager's code.
3. Pins first in test/pins-visibility.test.ts (committed before source changes, green at that commit): a plain park in a roomy measured row bounces back (F4); a useViewState cell set before a park is readable after the revive and a cell with a mounted listener is not dropped by the retain effect (viewState.ts:86-89, :137-147).
4. Owned differentials on a measured row where capacity binds: one per name in startVisibility, hideStickiness, hideWhenLast, capacityReturn, returnPriority, evictionRank, evictionOrder, viewState and tuning.saveQuietMs, tuning.gestureLockMs, tuning.returnTieBreak, tuning.dropTieBreak; the covered-name set equals those 12 (test/settings-enforcement-visibility.test.ts).
5. until-room, literal: hideStickiness until-room records freeAfterPark = capacity minus live park-capable count right after the park and the panel returns when free slots exceed it; [A B C] with capacity 3, hide B leaves it parked; open D then close D (free slots back to the threshold) leaves it parked; closing C or a capacity rise returns it; on an unmeasured row it holds like sticky until the first measurement, then takes the threshold.
6. tuning members and defaults: returnTieBreak is latest-first | earliest-first | row-order and dropTieBreak leftmost | rightmost (defaults latest-first and leftmost, equal to today's walks); on a literal row each member gives a different result; saveQuietMs and gestureLockMs from PanelsInput.tuning change the timers under fake timers (the save fires at the configured value; the gesture re-arms at it, read from a ref at each arm); the defaults equal 500 and 350 and the scaffold entry's corpus is unchanged.
7. S4 mutants (C5 with N = 6): the commit scan stops skipping held ids; hidePanel stops unstacking a capacity-parked panel; show or the gesture stops releasing the held mark; recordPark ignores capacityReturn manual; the until-room threshold ignored; wouldEmptyRow ignores the in-flight set. Each turns its named test red: a held panel stays parked in a roomy measured row; a capacity-parked panel stays parked after hide and after a close; a held panel can be shown again; a manual panel does not return when room opens; an until-room panel returns only after free slots exceed its threshold; hide A + hide B on [A B] leaves one present.
8. S1 property: at least 500 seeded sequences on measured rows with every panel park-policy assert after every settled commit liveParkable is at most capacity (capacity from computeRowLayout with the same inputs); the test prints and asserts 'sequences run: N' with N at least 500.
9. evictionRank, evictionOrder, returnPriority with literal expectations: a row of live panels with distinct ranks, opening one more parks the lowest rank; equal ranks park the farthest, nearest, leftmost or rightmost per the demander's evictionOrder (four literal ids); a capacity drop with all ranks equal parks exactly what fewestParksThatSettle returned before (200 seeded rows) and with distinct ranks parks the lower rank first at equal park counts; two parked panels and room for one return the higher returnPriority, ties follow each returnTieBreak value. A panel in the acted-on set is never parked by its own action.
10. Held ids are session-only: projectWorkspaceState output has no held key and a remount restores a parked panel parked. startVisibility parked: applyTemplate('create') writes live false and markHeld records the hold, so the panel is created parked, in panelList, not on the stack when sticky, on it when until-room, and not revealed by the first commit. hideWhenLast: wouldEmptyRow refuses only when no other panel is present (fixed columns count), one root-less case and one fixed-column case.
11. viewState latch, PanelRow mount: reset-on-park set before a park releases the value at that park and a revive returns the initial value; keep survives; flipped AFTER the park leaves the cell readable through the revive and releases it at the next park; an exiting mounted view keeps its value until it unmounts; mutant: the keep list reads the live setting instead of the latch; red.
12. Compat: the scaffold entry's corpus unchanged; `git diff -U0 -- test/panel-manager-collapse-decisions.test.ts | grep '^-' | grep -vc '^---'` prints 0 (the capacity-decision tests pass untouched). No new file named useProvide*; no useMemo or useCallback in new pointer code. C4: no value export and no export type added to index.ts; the api-report diff (etc/phonux.api.md) names exactly two additions, the PanelsInput members onNote and tuning, and nothing else: UsePanelManagerResult is not exported from index.ts and is not in the report (etc/phonux.api.md declares PanelsInput and ProvidedPanels and not UsePanelManagerResult, checked with `grep -c 'UsePanelManagerResult' etc/phonux.api.md` printing 0), so holdPanel, releaseHold, heldIds, wouldEmptyRow and markHeld never appear in it, and ProvidedPanels gains none of them in this entry (a diff line for any of them means an unplanned export and fails C4); check:api-report prints no warning of any kind (ae-forgotten-export, ae-missing-release-tag or any other). C2 and C3.
13. Constants rewired (the resolver entry created rowConstants.ts): useWorkspacePersistence.ts and useRowScrollGesture.ts import SAVE_QUIET_MS and GESTURE_LOCK_MS from rowConstants.ts and read the resolved values; the old name WORKSPACE_SAVE_QUIET_MS stays exported from useWorkspacePersistence.ts with the value 500 by one line after the import, `export { SAVE_QUIET_MS as WORKSPACE_SAVE_QUIET_MS };`, and the declaration `export const WORKSPACE_SAVE_QUIET_MS = 500;` (line 14 at 7a60e82) is deleted; nothing imports the old name (git grep at 7a60e82 finds it only in that file and in DESIGN.md line 585, which names it in prose and stays true); `grep -c 'GESTURE_LOCK_MS = ' panels/useRowScrollGesture.ts` prints 0 and `grep -c 'SAVE_QUIET_MS = 500' panels/useWorkspacePersistence.ts` prints 0 (each prints 1 at 7a60e82: the control), while `grep -c 'WORKSPACE_SAVE_QUIET_MS' panels/useWorkspacePersistence.ts` is at least 1 and the test asserts WORKSPACE_SAVE_QUIET_MS === LIBRARY_TUNING_DEFAULTS.saveQuietMs; test/rowconstants-drift.test.ts is edited to assert no second copy of these two numbers exists.
14. Where each setting's wiring lands (one file per key, so nothing is guessed): startVisibility: panelSettings.ts (applyTemplate('create') writes `live` from the resolved value) and panels/usePanelManager.ts (markHeld on every creation path); hideStickiness, hideWhenLast, capacityReturn, returnPriority, evictionRank, evictionOrder: panels/usePanelManager.ts (recordPark, the hold map, the commit scan, the park choice, the stack walk), reading each entry through effective() as panels/useProvidePanels.ts passes it; tuning.returnTieBreak and tuning.dropTieBreak: panels/usePanelManager.ts (the stack walk and the capacity drop), the value handed in by panels/useProvidePanels.ts from the resolved tuning; viewState: PanelRow.tsx (the keep list reads the latch set at each park commit); tuning.saveQuietMs: panels/useWorkspacePersistence.ts takes a new parameter saveQuietMs (default SAVE_QUIET_MS) from useProvidePanels and reads it from a ref at each arm; tuning.gestureLockMs: panels/useRowScrollGesture.ts takes a new parameter gestureLockMs (default GESTURE_LOCK_MS) from useProvidePanels, stores it in a ref assigned on every render like scrollRowRef, reads that ref when armUnlock arms the timer and never lists it as an effect dependency, so changing it does not tear the listeners down (the module constant GESTURE_LOCK_MS at line 16 of that file is deleted). A source check asserts the two refs and the absence of the old module constant.

### 5. phonux-engine-placement-and-motion-settings (phonux-lib)

**Enforce explicit placement, return and undo placement, the root marker, key-aware undo and motion settings**

Intent: Pure resolvePlacement for placeOnAdd, placeOnShow, placeOnReturn, placeFallback and undoPlacement (named positions, before, after, order, anchor; priority -1 for next-to); re-entry inside activate and both returns; explicit enter side that works root-less; the root marker and isPresent; layer, enterFrom, exitTo, closeExit, animated; key-aware undo.

Prerequisites: phonux-engine-visibility-settings

Touches:
- panelPlacement.ts
- panelWindow.ts
- panelRowEntry.ts
- panels/usePanelManager.ts
- panels/usePanelClosing.ts
- panels/useProvidePanels.ts
- PanelRow.tsx
- PanelRowSlot.tsx
- panelSettings.ts
- test/pins-placement.test.ts
- test/panel-placement.test.ts
- test/settings-enforcement-motion.test.ts
- test/key-aware-undo.test.ts
- test/source-invariants.test.ts
- test/row-shell-style.test.ts
- test/boundary.test.ts
- tools/mutants/phonux-engine-placement-and-motion-settings.json
- etc/*.api.md
- DESIGN.md

Evidence required:
1. C1 with F = 105. test/row-shell-style.test.ts is touched by additions only: `git diff -U0 -- test/row-shell-style.test.ts | grep '^-' | grep -vc '^---'` prints 0. New root files (panelPlacement.ts) raise MIN_LIBRARY_FILES in test/boundary.test.ts in the same commit. Every test in this entry that mounts useProvidePanels or usePanelManager builds its input through `panelsInput(...)` or `usePanelManagerWith(...)` from test/support/mount-harness.ts (the scaffold entry's helpers) and never writes a storedMaxPanels property or positional null itself; the retire-max-panels entry then edits that one file. Any input this entry adds to usePanelManager goes in one optional trailing options object (the first entry that needs one creates it; later entries add optional fields), never a new positional parameter, so test/support/mount-harness.ts's fixed call keeps typechecking without an edit here.
2. Interfaces this entry produces: panelPlacement.ts exports `resolvePlacement(row: readonly PanelWindow[], rootId: string, self: string | null, value: AddPlacement | 'in-place', o: { fallback: 'row-start' | 'row-end' | 'parkable-start' | 'parkable-end' | 'after-fixed'; order?: () => readonly string[] }): { anchor: AnchorRequest | null; notes: SettingNote[] }` (anchor null for in-place; every next-to value has priority -1) and `placePanel(row: readonly PanelWindow[], id: string, value, o): { row: PanelWindow[]; notes: SettingNote[] }`; notes go to PanelsInput.onNote (added by the visibility entry). The enter-side mark is `sideOfLiveRange` of the landing slot. panelRowEntry.ts exports `isPresent(p: { collapsible: boolean; live: boolean; marker?: boolean }): boolean`, true iff p is not a marker and is either not collapsible or live; PanelWindow and PanelRowEntry gain `marker?: true`. isPresent and resolvePlacement are internal (not in index.ts).
3. Pins first in test/pins-placement.test.ts (committed before source changes, green at that commit): computeRowLayout at fixedItemCount 0; computeRowOrder, movePanel, reanchorPanel and scrollRow with the root absent; PanelRow with rootId absent enters from right; PIN right-side tie: computeRowOrder of [H, A(H right 0), B(H right 1)] plus N(H right 0) is H A N B while N at priority -1 gives H N A B; PIN missing anchor: hydrate of a saved row [H S A B C D E] without S swaps C and D (literal output asserted); PIN positional anchors: deriveAnchorsFromOrder over [A, B] with the root id absent reads both { root, right } with priorities 0 and 1, over [A, R, B] with R the root reads A left and B right; PIN root marker: a row whose first entry is '__root__' passed as rootId keeps it in place across movePanel of an unrelated pair, reads entries after it right, and is drawn as a phone-width slot and counted by fixedViewCount today (flipped by the marker bullet below); KNOWN-BAD:2 after one close, two undoClose calls in one handler report the same log id twice through onPanelsRestored (precondition asserted: recentlyClosed non-null before both). `grep -rhoE "test\(.KNOWN-BAD:[0-9]" test | sort -u | wc -l` (distinct pin titles, so a pinned string repeated inside one pin counts once) prints 2 at that commit (this one and the close-order pin of the permission entry's file).
4. Placement table with exact id arrays written literally, never derived from the code under test. Row [H* A B]: row-start [N H* A B], row-end [H* A B N], parkable-start [H* N A B], parkable-end [H* A B N], after-fixed [H* N A B], {after:H} [H* N A B], {before:A} [H* N A B]. Row [P H* A B]: row-start [N P H* A B], row-end [P H* A B N], parkable-start [N P H* A B], parkable-end [P H* A B N], after-fixed [P H* N A B], {after:H} and {before:A} [P H* N A B]. Root-less [A B]: row-start [N A B], row-end [A B N], parkable-start [N A B], parkable-end [A B N], after-fixed [N A B], {before:A} [N A B], {after:H} the placeFallback. Plus order, anchor and in-place over those rows, [H*] alone and a row with only the new panel, each with A parked in a variant; executed === values x rows x variants (computed).
5. Pinned zone and marker: in [P H* A B] the anchor a new panel lands with is { root, left } under row-start and parkable-start and { root, right } under after-fixed, {after:H} and row-end; in a root-less row with the '__root__' marker row-start lands left of the marker and every other value right of it; mutant: parkable-start resolved like after-fixed; red.
6. Root marker, the hard gate for root-less rows. isPresent is the one reader of presence. In a root-less row [M, A, B] (M the '__root__' entry with marker true, A and B live scroll panels, phone 390, gap 16) PanelRow draws exactly two slots with leftOffsets [0, 406], widths [390, 390] and reservedWidth 796; usePanelNeighborWidths for A is { left: [], right: [390] }; fixedViewCount is 0; movePanel of B left gives [M, B, A] (one hop, over A, never over M) and of A left returns the same array; a drag never counts M as a hop; in a row with a root, History still counts 1 (the scaffold's goldens pass). Mutants: PanelRow filters by collapsible and live instead of isPresent; fixedViewCount counts the marker; each red.
7. Priority: { after: root } and { before: X } for a panel already present and for an appended one, on rows where the target already has a priority-0 neighbour on that side, land immediately next to the target; mutant: emit priority 0; red. row-end equals the 0.1.0 default { position: 'right' } by computeRowOrder id sequence over at least 200 seeded random rows (asserted >= 200); mutant: row-end resolved to the root's left; red.
8. Show, return and undo paths: placeOnShow runs through PanelsAPI.activate before promoteToLive; automatic returns keep their slot under the default placeOnReturn (pinned); row-end, row-start and { after: X } re-place a returning panel in the same update that makes it live, literal arrays for a rise that returns two panels; an undo uses undoPlacement (old-slot equals today's restorePanel index; row-end and { after: X } place the restored panel with its live flag kept; a missing target uses placeFallback) and a show uses placeOnShow, never placeOnReturn; a placement is never recorded as the person's override; mutant: placeOnReturn read by the show path; red.
9. Key-aware undo and the KNOWN-BAD:2 flip, mechanism stated: undoClose consumes its batch from closedRef (cleared synchronously) so a second call in the same handler finds nothing, reports only the ids it restored and skips a panel whose key is already present. KNOWN-BAD:2 now asserts the fixed behaviour under a name without the prefix; `grep -rhoE "test\(.KNOWN-BAD:[0-9]" test | sort -u | wc -l` (distinct pin titles, so a pinned string repeated inside one pin counts once) prints 1 (the close-order pin remains for the operations entry). Compat caller seeding { locked: true, collapsible: true }: an undo of a panel whose key is present reports nothing for it; the scaffold entry's corpus and typecheck tests pass. Mutant: undoClose reads the render-time closed; red.
10. Enter side: for each named value the explicit mark equals sideOfLiveRange of the landing slot; a mount test with rootId absent asserts the slot's initial x has the sign of that side. Fallbacks: a missing target, a self-naming target, order with no list and order with unknown ids all land at placeFallback and call onNote with a fallback note; placeOnAdd in-place throws invalid-settings.
11. enterFrom: left and right constants beat marks and the anchor formula; anchor equals the legacy anchoredEnter true in a PanelRow mount comparison; auto equals today's precedence. exitTo: constants beat exitRightIds while closingIds still wins. closeExit: closingIds exits by the setting (down default, left, right literal). animated false: panelRowSlotMotion returns a zero-duration transition; the default leaves PANEL_TRANSITION identical.
12. Source invariants: enterDirection reads enterFrom and anchoredEnter, never collapsible; zIndex reads layer only; the existing enterDirection invariant's retarget is the only edited expectation, named in the handoff; the single-line `zIndex={` and `enterDirection={` patterns stay findable by `lines.find`. One differential per name in placeOnAdd, placeOnShow, placeOnReturn, placeFallback, undoPlacement, layer, enterFrom, exitTo, closeExit, animated; covered set equals that 10 (test/settings-enforcement-motion.test.ts and test/panel-placement.test.ts).

C5 with N = 9: priority 0 instead of -1; row-start and parkable-start swapped; placeFallback ignored; enterFrom constant ignored; animated ignored; placeOnReturn ignored; undoClose reading the render-time closed; PanelRow reading collapsible instead of isPresent; fixedViewCount counting the marker. The entry that adds value exports edits the title and the sorted list in the same commit, renaming and never deleting the test: resolver 50, permissions 51, look tuning 54, keepMounted 55, row numbers 56, attach 60, header buttons 62; the release-candidate entry asserts 62 and edits no title. Every other entry leaves the file's title and list alone.

*Brief rule for every worker (both repos' CLAUDE.md):* comments say why, a constraint the code cannot show; no provenance (ticket or queue ids, dates, owner names); past-bug stories go to CHANGELOG.md or the commit message; design notes go in DESIGN.md, never a link to a tracker. Pins live in the entry that changes the behaviour, committed with its first tests; a flipped pin is renamed, never deleted.

### 6. phonux-engine-tuning-look-settings (phonux-lib)

**Resolve and thread the look tuning: row gap, close distances, slide timing, hop rule**

Intent: tuning.rowGap (workspace), tuning.dragToClose, tuning.slide and tuning.hopFraction become resolved keys with today's defaults; tuning.edgeScroll (48/900/500, owner-confirmed, a constant from the resolver entry) is only threaded through the slot context here. One resolved value per render reaches row, slots, shell, capacity and hops; DragToClose re-checks closability; its onClose becomes optional.

One value per render (PanelRow inside Workspace, rowGap 40): slot offsets, shell padding and capacity all use 40; changing the workspace tuning renders every consumer at the new value in the same render (a render log has no commit mixing 16 and 40, through useSyncExternalStore); PanelRow given a tuning prop that differs from its Workspace's context warns once in development (spy), equal values warn zero times.
8. dragToClose: shouldCloseOnRelease(measure, params = DRAG_CLOSE) moves its boundary at literal offsets for each of the six values (distanceFraction 0.5 on a 700 px box needs 350 px; minDistancePx 120 makes a 100 px drag not close; flickVelocity 1000 refuses an 800 px/s flick; flickMinDistancePx 60 refuses a 50 px flick at 900 px/s; cancelVelocity -100 cancels earlier); a DragToClose in a slot with a custom value closes at the custom boundary and one outside a row at the default; the app's five-number pin (app: test/drag-to-close.test.ts:41) still reads DRAG_CLOSE unchanged. Direction lock: 10 px horizontal locks horizontal at the default 8 and stays unlocked with directionLockPx 20; snap-back: a spy on motion's animate sees 520 and 42 by default and the slot's values when set.
9. slide: durations 0.4 default and 0.8 override, closeExitSeconds 0.18 default and 0.3 override shared by the slot's down exit and DragToClose's own exit (mutant: DragToClose reads DRAG_CLOSE.exitSeconds; red); animated false is still duration 0; PanelSizeBox and the shell's glide read the resolved slide. hopFraction: reorderStepsForOffset(offset, neighbors, gap, hopFraction) at 0.25, 0.5 and 0.75 over [phone, tablet] gives literal step counts and equals the pinned rows at 0.5 and with no argument; usePanelReorderSteps() inside a slot returns a function whose call (offsetX) gives the steps at gap 16 and 40 and hopFraction 0.5 and 0.25 without the view passing a gap or the neighbours; mutant: it uses GAP; red.

Cascade, scope, ranges: a per-panel dragToClose, slide or hopFraction beats the workspace value for that panel only; the workspace-only keys at template or instance yield wrong-scope; out-of-range leaves throw naming the leaf, both sides of every floor (the resolver entry's list). usePanelTuning inside a slot returns that panel's merged values (Object.keys asserted: rowGap, dragToClose, slide, hopFraction, edgeScroll); outside a row the library defaults; Workspace takes an optional tuning prop and provides it by context; ProvidedPanels.tuning carries the resolved workspace tuning.

C4: the barrel list gains exactly usePanelTuning, usePanelReorderSteps, slideToTransition and the barrel test's title and count change in the same commit to 'the phonux barrel exports exactly the 54 runtime values, no more, no fewer' (51 + 3; renamed, never deleted; test/phonux-barrel.test.ts is in Touches); the api report diff names two signature changes (reorderStepsForOffset gains an optional hopFraction, DragToClose.onClose becomes optional), the three new declarations of the C4 values, the optional tuning props of Workspace and PanelRow and ProvidedPanels.tuning, and nothing else; shouldCloseOnRelease also gains an optional params but is @internal and hidden from the barrel since 7a60e82, so it is absent from the report (`grep -c shouldCloseOnRelease etc/phonux.api.md` prints 0 before and after) and its change is proven by bullet 8 alone; no export type is added (PanelTuning and its siblings come from the resolver entry) and check:api-report prints no warning of any kind (ae-forgotten-export, ae-missing-release-tag or any other).

### 7. phonux-keep-mounted-render-path (phonux-lib)

**Add keepMounted: a parked view stays mounted, hidden, inert and out of layout**

Intent: keepMounted per panel: a parked view stays mounted in the same slot (display:none, inert, aria-hidden after its exit slide), decided by a latch at the park commit, mounted lazily at first show, re-entering from its enter side and never counted in layout or capacity. Adds usePanelHidden and Panel's capture rule. jsdom proves structure only.

Prerequisites: phonux-engine-visibility-settings, phonux-engine-tuning-look-settings

Touches:
- PanelRow.tsx
- PanelRowSlot.tsx
- Panel.tsx
- panelRowEntry.ts
- panelSettings.ts
- index.ts
- test/pins-render.test.ts
- test/keep-mounted.test.ts
- test/keep-mounted-lifetime.test.ts
- test/source-invariants.test.ts
- test/phonux-barrel.test.ts
- tools/mutants/phonux-keep-mounted-render-path.json
- etc/*.api.md
- DESIGN.md

Evidence required:
1. C1 with F = 52. Difficulty: hard (imperative slot controls, display:none from the animation promise, a latch; no precedent in the repo). Every test in this entry that mounts useProvidePanels or usePanelManager builds its input through `panelsInput(...)` or `usePanelManagerWith(...)` from test/support/mount-harness.ts (the scaffold entry's helpers) and never writes a storedMaxPanels property or positional null itself; the retire-max-panels entry then edits that one file.
2. Pin first in test/pins-render.test.ts (committed before source changes, green): a view with a mount-effect counter that starts parked and never mounted, shown then parked three times, gives 3 mounts and 3 unmounts after the park exits complete (from a live start it is 4 mounts and 3 unmounts; the test uses the parked start).
3. Owned differential for keepMounted, in test/keep-mounted.test.ts (the basename the configure matrix's bullet 9 requires; test/keep-mounted-lifetime.test.ts holds the lifetime cases), over the same three cycles: kept gives 1 mount and 0 unmounts, not kept 3 and 3; viewState survives in both.
4. Latch cases (PanelRow re-rendered with changed entry settings): (a) live then parked with keepMounted false (view unmounted), then true: the counter is unchanged until its next show and its next park keeps it; (b) flipped true during the park exit: the exiting view still unmounts; (c) latched kept then flipped false: it unmounts at once (zero-duration exit); (d) the viewState latch: reset-on-park flipped after a park drops no cell, before the park drops it at that park.
5. Hidden state: after the park exit completes the slot has display none, the inert attribute (hasAttribute('inert') asserted, React 19) and aria-hidden true; during the exit slide it has inert and is not display none; the revive commit has none of the three at its first layout effect; usePanelHidden is false while live (in view or scrolled out), false while exiting, true only when display is none, false outside a row. Fallback: animated:false, a zero-duration slide, reduced motion and an unchanged park target each reach display none without a timer (fake timers advanced by 0) because hidden follows the imperative animation's promise; mutant: hidden set from onAnimationComplete only; red.
6. Lazy mount: a restored-parked and a startVisibility-parked panel render no view (counter 0) until first shown; a panel that parks before ever being committed live never mounts. Lifetime: a close of a hidden kept panel unmounts it with a zero-duration exit; clearAll, a re-render with keepMounted false on a hidden panel and PanelRow unmount each unmount it; an undo remounts fresh with viewState intact; none of it is saved.
7. Motion values: a pure `parkExitTarget(rest, ownSpan, side, rowScroll)` returns the literal targets of a kept park exit (this entry always passes rowScroll false: left { x: -ownSpan, opacity: 0 }, right { x: rest + ownSpan, opacity: 0 }; the row-numbers entry passes the real flag and adds the rest-relative left target) plus the enter start for left and right; a revive whose exit side differs from its enter side sets x and opacity to the start before animating (a controls spy sees set before animate) and the slot's animate prop is absent while kept or reviving; mutant: the jump removed; red.
8. Layout independence (structure only): reservedWidth, leftOffsets, neighborWidthsAt and anchorIndexOf computed over present equal literal arrays for the same row without the kept entries (kept entries in the middle of the rendered list); over 200 seeded sequences capacity decisions and liveWidths are identical with keepMounted true and false.
9. Capture: Panel registers with the CaptureRegistry while visible and unregisters while hidden (a registry spy counts register and unregister); a CaptureTrigger spy sees exactly one capture at the park commit, issued before hidden; mutant: Panel stays registered while hidden; red. C6: viewState reset-on-park with keepMounted true resolves keepMounted false and PanelRow unmounts at park; a fixed column never takes the kept path. StrictMode: one logical mount and no leaked latch entry after unmount.
10. Default path unchanged: the scaffold entry's corpus, row-shell-style and source-invariants pass with zero removed lines; a PanelRow with no keepMounted entry renders markup string-equal to the scaffold entry's golden. C4: the barrel list gains exactly usePanelHidden and the barrel test's title and count change in the same commit to 'the phonux barrel exports exactly the 55 runtime values, no more, no fewer' (54 + 1; renamed, never deleted; test/phonux-barrel.test.ts is in Touches); no export type is added and check:api-report prints no warning of any kind (ae-forgotten-export, ae-missing-release-tag or any other). DESIGN.md states plainly that jsdom proves structure only and the app real-layout gate owns the rendering claims.
11. C5 with N = 6: hide via visibility:hidden; lazy mount removed; inert omitted during the exit; unmount on close skipped; enter-side jump removed; latch replaced by the live setting. C2 and C3.
12. Public doc comment that names a hidden value, in the file this entry already edits: PanelProps.viewId's doc in Panel.tsx (line 58 at 7a60e82, 'see snapshots.ts's CaptureRegistryContext') now names CaptureRegistryProvider, the exported way to supply a registry. Check: `git grep -nE '^[[:space:]]*(/[*][*]|[*]|//).*CaptureRegistryContext' -- Panel.tsx | wc -l` prints 1 at 7a60e82 (control) and 0 after, and `git grep -c CaptureRegistryProvider -- Panel.tsx` is at least 1; the code uses of the context in Panel.tsx stay, because only comment lines are scanned; the edit changes no line of etc/phonux.api.md.

### 8. phonux-retire-max-panels-members (phonux-lib)

**Retire PanelsInput.storedMaxPanels and the four SettingsAPI max-panels members (breaking; effectiveMaxPanels takes two arguments)**

Intent: Breaking, the design's own choice (section 10), listed for review in Jojos-Creations/phonux#2: remove PanelsInput.storedMaxPanels and SettingsAPI.maxPanelsOnScreen, fitCount, minPanelsOnScreen and setMaxPanelsOnScreen (createFakeHost too); effectiveMaxPanels becomes (fitCount, fixedColumnCount). settingsColumn stays. No new behaviour; the row-numbers entry builds on the new signature.

Prerequisites: phonux-engine-visibility-settings, phonux-engine-placement-and-motion-settings, phonux-engine-tuning-look-settings, phonux-keep-mounted-render-path

Touches:
- panelCapacity.ts
- hostApi.tsx
- fakeHost.ts
- panelRowLayout.ts
- panels/usePanelManager.ts
- panels/useProvidePanels.ts
- tools/phonux-pack-smoke.mjs
- test/pins-retire-max-panels.test.ts
- test/panel-manager-collapse-decisions.test.ts
- test/panel-layout.test.ts
- test/support/mount-harness.ts
- test/compat-0.1.0.typecheck.ts
- tools/mutants/phonux-retire-max-panels-members.json

Evidence required:
1. C1 with F = 3: BASE from tools/testcount.sh before any edit, on phonux-lib main at 7a60e82 or later (`git merge-base --is-ancestor 7a60e82 HEAD` exits 0). AFTER >= BASE + 3 = the four new test() cases of test/pins-retire-max-panels.test.ts (bullets 2, 7 and 9: P1 effectiveMaxPanels pins, P2 fake-host settings keys, P3 CHANGELOG names, P4 retired-names source check) minus the one existing test deleted whole in bullet 6 (D = 1). Difficulty: medium (mechanical and breaking, about 15 source and test files, no render path). No useMemo or useCallback is added, no new root file (test/boundary.test.ts and test/phonux-barrel.test.ts have empty diffs).
2. Pins first (committed before any source change, green at the base), test/pins-retire-max-panels.test.ts. P1 effectiveMaxPanels, three-argument form (stored, fitCount, fixedColumnCount): (3, null, 2) is Infinity, (null, 5, 2) is 5, (5, 3, 2) is 3, (1, 1, 3) is 4. P2: Object.keys(createFakeHost().settings) deep-equals ['maxPanelsOnScreen', 'fitCount', 'minPanelsOnScreen', 'setMaxPanelsOnScreen', 'settingsColumn'] (fakeHost.ts:51-60). The flip commit renames and rewrites them, never deletes them: P1 becomes the two-argument cases (null, 2) Infinity, (5, 2) 5, (1, 3) 4 (the (5, 3, 2) case tested only the stored cap and goes), P2 becomes ['settingsColumn']. The pin file holds no typecheck probe; the type-level probes live only in test/compat-0.1.0.typecheck.ts (bullet 6), so the grep of bullet 4 can exclude that one file alone.
3. Interfaces. panelCapacity.ts: `effectiveMaxPanels(fitCount: number | null, fixedColumnCount: number): number` is Infinity for a null fit count and otherwise max(fixedColumnCount + 1, fitCount); it stays @internal and out of index.ts. panels/usePanelManager.ts loses its storedMaxPanels parameter (panels, setPanels, phoneWidth, containerWidth, fixedColumnCount, rootId); its capacity reads the fit count and the fixed-column floor only. PanelsInput loses `storedMaxPanels` and useProvidePanels passes nothing in its place. SettingsAPI (hostApi.tsx) keeps exactly `settingsColumn` and createFakeHost().settings holds only it. The overflow-driven parking comments at hostApi.tsx:157, :166, :176-179 and :192 are not touched here: the row-numbers entry rewords them with the behaviour.
4. Retired surface: `git grep -nE 'maxPanelsOnScreen|minPanelsOnScreen|setMaxPanelsOnScreen|storedMaxPanels|SettingsAPI.{1,5}fitCount|settings[.]fitCount|readonly fitCount' -- . ':!CHANGELOG.md' ':!test/fixtures' ':!test/compat-0.1.0.typecheck.ts' ':!test/pins-retire-max-panels.test.ts' ':!dist' ':!node_modules'` prints nothing, covering the root files, panels/, tools/, test/, etc/ and DESIGN.md, while the same command at 7a60e82 (the pattern and pathspec after the revision) prints more than 0 (control that it read the right tree). The same file exports the pure internal `maxScrollOf(n: RowNumbers): number` = max(0, rowMarginLeft + stripWidth - windowWidth) (0 when windowWidth is Infinity): the farthest legal scrollLeft, computed from the numbers and never read back from the DOM; it is not in index.ts. rowScroll is true iff any present entry carries settings whose overflow is 'scroll'; windowWidth = containerWidth - 2 x gap (Infinity when containerWidth is 0); leftOffsets[k] = the sum of the earlier widths plus one gap each; reservedWidths = the widths of present non-park panels.

### 10. phonux-row-scroll-gesture-and-requests (phonux-lib)

**Scroll to a panel: the scroller, scroll-on-open and follow, the step gesture, reveal on scroll and initial scroll**

Intent: RowScroller (the one writer of scrollLeft: glides with the 6B cancel rules, an exclusive claim), scrollTargetFor and stepTarget, scrollOnOpen and scrollOnMove requests, the step, free and off row gesture, revealOnScroll and initialScroll after the restore settles. Workspace marks its container data-row-scroller. Names the seam a later custom scrollbar uses.

Prerequisites: phonux-row-numbers-and-overflow

Touches:
- rowScroller.ts
- scrollTargets.ts
- panels/useRowScrollGesture.ts
- panels/useWorkspacePersistence.ts
- panels/useProvidePanels.ts
- panels/usePanelManager.ts
- Workspace.tsx
- PanelRow.tsx
- panelWindow.ts
- panelSettings.ts
- test/pins-scroll.test.ts
- test/scroll-targets.test.ts
- test/row-scroller.test.ts
- test/row-scroll-mount.test.ts
- test/row-gesture.test.ts
- test/settings-enforcement-scroll.test.ts
- test/source-invariants.test.ts
- test/phonux-barrel.test.ts
- test/boundary.test.ts
- tools/mutants/phonux-row-scroll-gesture-and-requests.json
- etc/*.api.md
- DESIGN.md

Evidence required:
1. C1 with F = 66. Difficulty: hard (executor, glide and gesture). No useMemo or useCallback in new pointer code; no flushSync import in the new scroll files (a planted import turns the source check red). New root files (rowScroller.ts, scrollTargets.ts) raise MIN_LIBRARY_FILES in test/boundary.test.ts in the same commit.
2. Interfaces (1/2). rowScroller.ts (internal, not in the barrel: a barrel test fails on any export name matching scroller, with a scratch-export control): `rowScrollerFor(element: HTMLElement): RowScroller`, one object per element and the same object on every call, with `RowScroller = { scrollLeft(): number; glideTo(target: number, o: { durationSec: number; instant: boolean }): boolean; glideTarget(): number | null; stop(): void; claim(): ScrollClaim | null }` and `ScrollClaim = { write(left: number): number; setMaxScroll(max: number): void; release(): void }`; `closestRowScroller(from: Element): HTMLElement | null` returns the nearest ancestor-or-self carrying the data-row-scroller attribute, which Workspace sets on its scroll container, and never looks at computed overflow (so it also finds a container whose overflow-x is hidden under rowGesture 'off'). No bound is read from the DOM: scrollTargetFor and stepTarget take maxScroll from maxScrollOf(computeRowNumbers(...)), and a claim is bounded only by what setMaxScroll last gave it (a write before any setMaxScroll applies nothing, so an uninformed claim cannot run away). glideTo returns false and does nothing while a claim is held; claim() returns null while one is held; write clamps to [0, the bound from setMaxScroll] and returns the applied delta, and the scroller counts the claim holder's writes as its own.
3. Interfaces (2/2). scrollTargets.ts: `scrollTargetFor(i: { left: number; width: number; scrollLeft: number; clientWidth: number; maxScroll: number; gap: number; align: ScrollAlign }): number` (left = gap + rowMarginLeft + leftOffsets[k] with the TARGET margin of the commit; the window is scrollLeft + gap to scrollLeft + clientWidth - gap; clamped to [0, maxScroll], the strip's bound from the row numbers); `stepTarget(direction: 'left' | 'right', base: number, panelStarts: readonly number[], maxScroll: number): number | null`; `gestureTarget(i: { direction: 'left' | 'right'; base: number; clientWidth: number; maxScroll: number; liveStarts: readonly number[]; liveWidths: readonly number[]; gap: number; hidden: readonly { id: string; liveBefore: number; held: boolean; policy: 'edges' | 'any' | 'never' }[] }): { kind: 'show'; id: string } | { kind: 'scroll'; to: number } | { kind: 'none' }`. ProvidedPanels gains `scrollTo(id: string, align?: ScrollAlign): boolean` (true when a request was recorded) and `followPanel(id: string): void` (records a scrollOnMove request with a was-in-view flag; the raw reanchor and setFormFactor wrappers call it, and so do the manager verbs of the operations and pin entries); useWorkspacePersistence returns `{ restoreSettled: boolean }` (true at once without storage).
4. Pins first in test/pins-scroll.test.ts (committed before source changes, green): with [A, B parked, C] and A, C live, scrollRow in both directions reveals nothing; a swipe on a row whose parked panels lie beyond the live range swaps (A parks, the next parked returns), one step per burst under the 350 ms lock, arrow keys with no lock; activate of a live panel is a same-array no-op; the container has one wheel listener and window one keydown listener. The swap pins flip (renamed) when the gesture scrolls.
5. scrollTargetFor and stepTarget, literal (gap 16, phone 390, eleven phone panels, rowMarginLeft 0, so left = 16 + 406k, clientWidth 1000, maxScroll 3482 = maxScrollOf of the eleven-panel row): panel 8 (left 3264) from scrollLeft 0 gives into-view 2670, start 3248, center 2959; panel 10 (left 4076) start clamps to 3482; panel 1 (left 422) at scrollLeft 0 is unchanged under into-view; panel 0 at scrollLeft 1000 gives 0 and panel 2 (left 828) gives 812. stepTarget over panelStarts k x 406 (k 0 to 10): from 0 right 406, from 406 right 812, from 3248 right 3482 (clamped), from 3482 right null, from 812 left 406, from 0 left null, from 3482 left 3248. Mutants: into-view ignores the right padding; step uses the nearest panel centre; each red.

RowScroller: a glide to 812 from 0 with durationSec 0.4, then 0.8, ends at 812 after the matching time on the scaffold's virtual frame clock and not before; instant moves at once and is what animated:false and reduced motion give; a glide stops at pointer-down in the container (scrollLeft stays where it was) and on a scroll it did not write (a 2 px foreign change stops it, a 1 px one does not); a second glideTo retargets from the current value; while a claim is held glideTo returns false and claim() returns null, after release both work; after setMaxScroll(3482) write(5000) returns the delta to 3482 whatever the DOM reports (a stubbed scrollWidth of 99999 does not raise it), and a write before any setMaxScroll applies nothing; a mounted Workspace carries data-row-scroller on its scroll container and on no other element, whether rowScroll is true or false; closestRowScroller finds the marked container from a deep descendant, returns null with no mark, and finds it when its overflow-x is hidden; rowScrollerFor(el) is identical for one element and different for another.
7. scrollOnOpen (mount test, container geometry stubbed with literal numbers, the executor observed through a spy on glideTo), only through the paths that exist at this entry: a Host-bag create (the raw PanelsAPI.create), activate of a live panel out of view, activate of a parked or held panel after releaseHold (the path a History click takes), an undo restoring a live panel and ProvidedPanels.scrollTo each record one request and scroll to the literal target; into-view on a panel already in view records none (changed:false); none leaves scrollLeft unchanged; first paint, restore, an automatic return and a panel created hidden record none (and no waiting-request note); two requests in one commit: the last wins; a request for a panel not yet present runs at the commit that adds it, else drops with a note after two commits. Every literal-target case sets scrollOnOpen explicitly ('into-view', 'start' and 'center' each); one default case computes its expected target with scrollTargetFor, its align read from LIBRARY_PANEL_DEFAULTS.scrollOnOpen, so a changed default is a one-value edit. The scroll requests of addPanel after ready belong to the attach entry, open() and show() to the operations entry, and a pin with pinningWhenNotLive 'show' and a deferred pin applied by a show to the pin entry; each of those entries pins its own. The glide uses tuning.slide.durationSec (0.4 and 0.8) and is instant under animated:false and reduced motion.
8. scrollOnMove and the claim: followPanel through raw reanchor and setFormFactor on an in-view panel ends inside the window (literal scrollLeft); an out-of-view panel records none; scrollOnMove none records none; a drag release records none. One default case reads its expectation from LIBRARY_PANEL_DEFAULTS.scrollOnMove; every other case sets scrollOnMove explicitly. While a claim is held a recorded scrollOnOpen, scrollOnMove or scrollTo request is dropped with the runtime note scroll-dropped-during-drag (spy on onNote) and scrollTo returns false; the same request in the commit after the release runs.
9. Gesture and glide: rowGesture step: a wheel burst of 30 events scrolls exactly one stepTarget and the glide it started is not cancelled by the burst's later events (the lock consumes them); a second burst inside tuning.gestureLockMs does nothing and one after it moves again; Shift+deltaY acts as horizontal, plain deltaY does not preventDefault; 10 ArrowRight repeats advance 10 stops (the base is the in-flight target); arrows skip INPUT, TEXTAREA, SELECT, contentEditable targets and any modifier, and still step with a button focused; at the strip end nothing happens. A glide stops on pointer-down and on a scroll event whose scrollLeft differs from the glide's last value by more than 1 px (spy). free and off install no listener (count 0; positive control: step installs 2). The gesture does nothing while a claim is held (a wheel burst and arrow presses leave scrollLeft unchanged).
10. revealOnScroll with [A, B hidden, C] and A in view: edges reveals a HELD B in place and scrolls it into view in both directions (Q1), an engine-parked B only when no live panel lies beyond the window on that side, any reaches it, never skips it and steps to the next live panel start; a History click (activate after releaseHold, the path the manager's show() will take) brings B back under all three values; the default case reads revealOnScroll from LIBRARY_PANEL_DEFAULTS and every other case sets it. With [view][L1 L2 L3][hidden H], a swipe toward H steps over L1, L2 and L3 in turn and reveals H only when no live panel lies between the window edge and H (literal scrollLeft values); a held H is never reached by skipping live panels.
11. initialScroll: start, end, root and { panel: id } (a missing id gives start) each apply once (the default case sets no tuning and expects the meaning of LIBRARY_TUNING_DEFAULTS.initialScroll, computed in the test, 0 for 'start'; every other case sets it explicitly), without animation, to literal scrollLeft values (the row is History, 5 pinned panels left of it and 5 web panels right of it, eleven panels at container 1000: stripWidth 4450, windowWidth 968, maximum scrollLeft 3482; 'root' is 2030, which is below that maximum, and 'end' is 3482, the maximum itself and the value phonux-app ships (the owner's launch position); a row of only History and 5 pinned panels has maximum 1452 and would clamp, so no case uses it for 2030; one extra case at that six-panel row asserts the clamp, 'root' gives 1452). Timing: with a deferred storage.load() that resolves later and 5 saved pinned panels and 5 saved web panels (the same eleven-panel row), 'root' does NOT run on the first measured commit (scrollLeft stays 0 while only History exists) and runs after the restore has settled, putting History's left edge at the window start (scrollLeft 2030); the same deferred-load row with 'end' stays at scrollLeft 0 on the first measured commit and ends at 3482 after the restore, with the two newest web panels (left edges 3670 and 4076) inside the window [3482, 4482] and History (right edge 2436) outside it; with no storage it runs at the first measured commit; a wheel or pointer-down before the restore settles makes it skip; a later re-measure or restore does not scroll; a root-less row falls back to start.

Seam for a later custom scrollbar (Q18, deferred, not an entry): the three facts it relies on are tests: rowScrollerFor returns one object per element, claim() is exclusive, and write moves scrollLeft with the scroller's own last-value bookkeeping so the glide rule never mistakes the claim holder's writes for foreign scrolls; DESIGN.md names the seam in one paragraph (RowScroller, computeRowNumbers' windowWidth and stripWidth, claim, a workspace tuning key to switch it on) and lists custom scrollbar under deferred; grep -c 'custom scrollbar' DESIGN.md is at least 1.
13. One differential per name revealOnScroll, scrollOnOpen, scrollOnMove, tuning.rowGesture, tuning.initialScroll; covered set equals those five. C4: no value export (the barrel list is unchanged) and no export type added (ScrollAlign comes from the resolver entry); the api report diff shows ProvidedPanels scrollTo and followPanel and nothing else, and check:api-report prints no warning of any kind (ae-forgotten-export, ae-missing-release-tag or any other).

### 11. phonux-edge-scroll-while-dragging (phonux-lib)

**Scroll the row while a dragged panel is held at the window's edge (scrollOnDrag, tuning.edgeScroll)**

Intent: Q19: a horizontal mouse or pen reorder drag held in an edge band scrolls the row, bounded by the strip: pure edgeScrollSpeed and edgeScrollStep, DragToClose claiming the row scroller, the box and released offset carrying the scroll distance and landing on its slot, reduced-motion steps, drag-to-close and the hop rule unchanged, the 6B cancel rules. Per panel: scrollOnDrag, tuning.edgeScroll.

Prerequisites: phonux-row-scroll-gesture-and-requests, phonux-engine-tuning-look-settings

Touches:
- edgeScroll.ts
- dragToClose.tsx
- panelSlotContext.ts
- PanelRow.tsx
- test/edge-scroll-pure.test.ts
- test/edge-scroll-drag.test.ts
- test/edge-scroll-cancel.test.ts
- test/settings-enforcement-edge-scroll.test.ts
- test/source-invariants.test.ts
- test/boundary.test.ts
- tools/mutants/phonux-edge-scroll-while-dragging.json
- DESIGN.md

Evidence required:
1. C1 with F = 56. Difficulty: hard (a pointer loop, a claim and a transform in one component). No useMemo or useCallback in new pointer code; no flushSync import; the frame loop runs on requestAnimationFrame only (a source check with a planted setInterval control). New root files (edgeScroll.ts) raise MIN_LIBRARY_FILES in test/boundary.test.ts in the same commit.
2. Interfaces this entry produces. edgeScroll.ts (pure, no React): `edgeScrollSpeed(i: { pointerX: number; left: number; right: number; zonePx: number; maxPxPerSec: number }): number` (signed px/s, negative toward the left edge, 0 outside both bands) and `edgeScrollStep(i: { speed: number; dtMs: number; scrollLeft: number; maxScroll: number }): number` (the clamped delta of one frame: speed x min(dtMs, 50) / 1000, kept inside [0, maxScroll]). panelSlotContext.ts (the permission entry's leaf) gains `dragScroll: { mode: 'edge' | 'none'; zonePx: number; maxPxPerSec: number; reducedStepMs: number; instant: boolean; maxScroll: number } | undefined`, which PanelRow builds from effective(p).scrollOnDrag, the merged tuning.edgeScroll (usePanelTuning, look-tuning entry), animated and maxScrollOf of its row numbers (the strip's bound; undefined outside a row and when rowScroll is false). DragToClose and ReorderHandle keep their props and signatures; the scroller is rowScrollerFor(closestRowScroller(box)) found by the data-row-scroller mark (the scroller entry), never by computed overflow, with claim(), setMaxScroll() and write() from the scroller entry. The claim is taken at the axis lock and bounded by dragScroll.maxScroll at the claim and again on every commit (never by scrollWidth, which a transformed descendant inflates), the scroll distance counts from scrollLeft at pointer-down, and the box is drawn at Motion's drag x plus a second MotionValue (the scroll distance) rendered as the CSS translate property, which Motion's rewrites of transform never touch. Edge scrolling applies to mouse and pen drags only: the grab handle is touch-action pan-x, so a touch swipe is a native pan and ends in pointercancel.
3. edgeScrollSpeed literals (container left 0, right 1000, zonePx 48, maxPxPerSec 900): pointer 500 gives 0; 48 gives 0; 47 gives -18.75; 24 gives -450; 0 gives -900; -50 gives -900; 952 gives 0; 953 gives 18.75; 976 gives 450; 1000 gives 900; 1500 gives 900; a container from 0 to 80 gives zones of 40: pointer 20 gives -450, 40 gives 0, 60 gives 450. edgeScrollStep literals: speed 900, dtMs 20 gives 18; dtMs 500 gives 45 (the 50 ms cap; every call passes its numbers explicitly, none reads a default); speed 900 with scrollLeft equal to maxScroll gives 0; speed -900 at scrollLeft 0 gives 0; at scrollLeft 3470 with maxScroll 3482, speed 900 and dtMs 20 gives 12. Mutants: no 50 ms cap; the zone measured from the window instead of the container; each red.
4. Mount test (jsdom, container geometry stubbed: clientWidth 1000; the scaffold's virtual frame clock at 20 ms frames; eleven scroll panels, phone 390, gap 16; maxScroll 3482 from the row numbers): pointer-down on a web panel's grab bar, a 40 px move right (the axis locks x), then held at clientX 1000 for 100 frames (the first frame has dt 0): scrollLeft after 100 frames equals 99 x edgeScrollStep({ speed: maxPxPerSec, dtMs: 20, ... }) with maxPxPerSec and zonePx read from LIBRARY_TUNING_DEFAULTS.edgeScroll (900 and 48 today, so 99 x 18 = 1782: the numbers bullet 3 pins with explicit parameters); held at clientX 500 it stays 0 (positive control: the run at 1000 moves); on release onDragEnd receives 40 + that distance (1822 today) and the view's reorderStepsForOffset over five 390 neighbours gives the hops the literal thresholds 203, 609, 1015, 1421 and 1827 give (4 today); with scrollOnDrag none the same gesture leaves scrollLeft 0 and gives 0 hops (the differential for that name). The drawn position of the box (transform x plus the translate property) equals the pointer offset plus the scroll distance at every frame. The runaway bound: the container's scrollWidth is a getter returning the stub 4482 plus the box's current transform and translate, standing in for the transformed descendant that CSS counts into scrollable overflow; held at clientX 1000 for 400 frames (8 s, longer than the strip takes) scrollLeft ends at exactly 3482, never above, and stays there; mutant: the bound is scrollWidth minus clientWidth; scrollLeft keeps rising past 3482 and the test goes red. The real-layout version of this probe is the real-layout gate's.
5. Reduced motion (matchMedia stubbed to reduce) and animated false each give the same result: no continuous scroll; with the pointer held at clientX 1000 for four reduced-motion intervals (reducedStepMs read from LIBRARY_TUNING_DEFAULTS.edgeScroll, 500 ms today, in 20 ms frames plus one) the row jumps once per interval, each jump instant and scrollLeft unchanged between jumps, to the panel starts 406, 812, 1218 and 1624, and onDragEnd receives 40 + 1624 = 1664 (4 hops); the box's two translation components jump together; a pointer that leaves the band before one interval causes no jump; half the interval doubles the jump count; held for twelve intervals the row ends at 3482 (the last panel start clamped to the strip's bound) and stops there.
6. Drag-to-close unaffected: a vertical-locked drag (dy 120, dx 5) held at clientX 1000 for 100 frames leaves scrollLeft 0 and closes by shouldCloseOnRelease exactly as without this entry (onClose once at the boundaries the look-tuning entry's shouldCloseOnRelease tests pin, not called below them); a horizontal-locked edge-scroll drag released after a 300 px vertical wobble never calls onClose; a closable:false panel (reorder only) still edge-scrolls and reorders; DragToClose without reorder never scrolls; the look-tuning entry's DragToClose tests pass with zero removed lines.
7. Hop rule unchanged: the library never calls reorderStepsForOffset (a source check: no import of it in dragToClose.tsx or edgeScroll.ts, with a planted-import control); a released offset of 1750 over five 390 neighbours gives 5 hops at tuning.hopFraction 0.25 and 4 at 0.5, and 1500 gives 4 at 0.5 and 3 at 0.75; nothing hops during the drag (no onDragEnd before the release).
8. 6B cancel rules and edge cases: a glide started by scrollOnOpen is in flight when the pointer goes down on the grab bar: it stops there (scrollLeft stays, no later write); while the drag holds the claim a scrollOnOpen, scrollOnMove or ProvidedPanels.scrollTo request is dropped with the note scroll-dropped-during-drag (scrollTo returns false) and the same request recorded in the release commit runs; the claim is released at release, pointercancel, unmount of the panel and rowScroll turning false (claim() returns non-null after each) and the row stays where the pointer left it (no snap, no glide after release). A pointercancel mid-drag, which a native pan sends, releases the claim with no scroll write and no hop, and the grab handle's sx has touchAction 'pan-x' (so edge scroll is mouse and pen only). Native scrolls during the hold differ by mode: under rowGesture 'free' a foreign scrollLeft change of 300 is added to (the final scrollLeft is 300 plus the edge distance and the drawn position includes the 300); under 'step' a 600-delta wheel burst scrolls nothing; under 'off' nothing scrolls natively either. Under 'off' a held drag still edge-scrolls by code (scrollLeft rises as under 'step'), because the scroller is found by its mark, not by overflow. Claim already held: a claim taken first by test code (standing for a second drag) makes this drag take no edge scroll (scrollLeft unchanged, the box still follows the pointer, onDragEnd still reports the pointer offset, a vertical-locked release still closes). Baseline: the scroll distance counts from scrollLeft at pointer-down, so a native scroll of 100 under 'free' between pointer-down and the axis lock is counted: onDragEnd receives the pointer offset plus 100 plus the edge distance.
9. Release landing (the box ends on its slot): at release the two transform components (Motion's x, and the scroll MotionValue) both animate to 0 in the same call with the same options while the slot's own x animates to its new rest (a spy on animate sees both calls in one release); under reduced motion both jump together; with the scroll distance 1782 and the 4-hop release above, and with a scroll distance above 0 and no hop, after the virtual clock passes the snap-back the box's transform is 'none' and its translate property reads zero (empty, none or 0px 0px); mutant: the scroll MotionValue is not zeroed at release; the settled transform assertion goes red. The real landing (rect.left equal to the new slot's rest within 1 px, no jump above the pointer offset between frames) is the real-layout gate's.
10. One differential per name scrollOnDrag and tuning.edgeScroll (the pointer sits at half the default zonePx from the edge; zonePx set to a quarter of the default against the default: no scroll against scroll; maxPxPerSec at half the default halves the distance; all values derived from LIBRARY_TUNING_DEFAULTS.edgeScroll, both through the slot context); covered set equals those two.
11. Source invariants: the loop reads only the slot context's dragScroll, never collapsible; DragToClose installs no listener while no drag is held (listener counts before and after, with a control); no useMemo or useCallback in the loop code. C4: no value export and no prop change; git diff --stat -- etc is empty.
12. C5 with N = 10: no 50 ms cap; the zone measured from the window; the released offset omits the scroll distance; the drawn x omits it; the claim never released at pointercancel; the loop runs while the axis lock is y; reduced-motion jumps every frame; the bound read from scrollWidth minus clientWidth; the scroll MotionValue not zeroed at release; the scroller found by computed overflow. C2 and C3.

### 12. phonux-saved-overrides-and-template-paths (phonux-lib)

**Saved shape, overrides, lenient restore and the template creation paths**

Intent: persist, rememberLayout, restoreVisibility; saved keys key, instance, overrides (anchor, tier, parkable), applied, closable, root, dismissed; sanitizer, lenient restore, one pure reconcile, a minimal template registry, session-anchor re-derive, dormant overrides, the recording functions recordOverride and clearOverrides, Host-bag create through templates. A 0.1.0 file restores unchanged.

Prerequisites: phonux-engine-permission-settings, phonux-engine-visibility-settings, phonux-engine-placement-and-motion-settings, phonux-engine-tuning-look-settings, phonux-keep-mounted-render-path, phonux-row-numbers-and-overflow, phonux-row-scroll-gesture-and-requests, phonux-edge-scroll-while-dragging

Touches:
- workspaceState.ts
- workspacePlan.ts
- panels/useWorkspacePersistence.ts
- panels/useProvidePanels.ts
- panels/usePanelManager.ts
- panelWindow.ts
- viewRegistry.tsx
- panelSettings.ts

- test/source-invariants.test.ts
- test/boundary.test.ts
- tools/mutants/phonux-saved-overrides-and-template-paths.json
